Let a_i ∈ {0,1}. Use ±1 s_i=2a_i-1, mean 0. For lag k>0, B_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} a_i(1-a_{i+k}). C_k = ∑(1-a_i)a_{i+k}. B_k = (n-k - ∑ s_i s_{i+k} - ∑(s_i - s_{i+k})?)/4. Compute: 1-a_j = (1-s_j)/2, a_i=(1+s_i)/2. Product=(1+s_i-s_j-s_i s_j)/4. Sum over i=0..L-1, L=n-k. B_k = [L + S_i - S_{i+k} - R_k]/4, where S_i=∑_{i=0}^{L-1}s_i, S_{i+k}=∑_{i=k}^{n-1}s_i, R_k=∑_{i=0}^{L-1}s_i s_{i+k}. Since total ∑ s=0, S_i = -∑_{i=L}^{n-1}s_i = -tail after n-k, S_{i+k}=∑_{i=k}^{n-1}s_i. Difference S_i - S_{i+k}= -tail(k)?? Let T_k = ∑_{i=n-k}^{n-1}s_i (last k), U_k = ∑_{i=0}^{k-1}s_i (first k). S_i (last n-k sum) = -T_k. S_{i+k} (from k to end sum) = -U_k. So S_i - S_{i+k} = -T_k + U_k = U_k - T_k. Thus B_k=(L + U_k - T_k - R_k)/4. C_k=(L - U_k + T_k - R_k)/4. So max(B,C)=L/4 - R_k/4 + |U_k-T_k|/4.

Implement `weighted_gradient(h, weights)`:
- `grad=np.zeros(n)`
- For each idx,w in enumerate(weights) if w>1e-10:
   k=idx-(n-1)
   if k>=0:
      # first term: H[i] shifted first? i-k = n? formula: contribution (1-H[i-k]) for i from k..n-1.
      grad[k:] += w * (1 - h[:n-k])  # i-k from 0..n-k-1 gives H[i-k]
      # second: -H[i+k] for i=0..n-k-1
      grad[:n-k] -= w * h[k:]
   else:
      p=-k
      # i-k = i+p valid for i=0..n-p-1? Since i-k=i+p <n -> i<n-p
      grad[:n-p] += w * (1 - h[p:])  # H[i+p]
      # second: -H[i+k]= -H[i-p] valid for i>=p
      grad[p:] -= w * h[:n-p]

`def normalize_zero_sum(d): d=d-d.mean(); return d`

`def line_search(h, d, base_g=None, grid=512):`
- d = d.copy(); if `np.max(np.abs(d)) < 1e-14`: return h, c.

- Ensure d small scale? If max abs >2, cap? For projected/proj FW, d in [-1,1]. Fine.
- If base_g is None: `base_g=compute_g(h)`.
- `U=1-h`
- `g1 = np.correlate(d, U, 'full') - np.correlate(h, d, 'full')`
- `g2 = np.correlate(d, d, 'full')`
- `dx=2/n`
- Base C = max(base_g)*dx.
- `lams = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, GRID)`
- For each lambda? Vectorize: `vals = base_g[None,:] + g1[None,:]*lams[:,None] - g2[None,:]*(lams[:,None]**2)`. For n=250, 512*499=256k; good. `cvals = vals.max(axis=1)*dx`. Find min. - Return h + lam_best*d, best_c.

`def top_weights(g, K, temp):`
- g counts. `maxg = g.max()`.
- If K >= len, idx all. But top K: `idx=np.argpartition(g, -K)[-K:]` maybe; sort not needed. For stability, use K min(K,len(g)). We can compute `gs=g[idx]`; if temp<=0: weights uniform len K. Else `w=np.exp((gs-maxg)/temp)`. Normalize. Return full weights zeros with w at idx? For gradient loops, we can just return list of (idx, weight) to avoid length 2n allocation.
```
def top_weight_items(g, K, temp):
   maxg=float(g.max())
   if K >= g.size: idx=np.arange(g.size)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(g, g.size-K)[-K:]
   gs=g[idx]
   if temp <= 0: w=np.full_like(gs, 1.0/gs.size) dtype float
   else: w=np.exp((gs-maxg)/temp); w/=w.sum()
   return [(int(idx[i]), float(w[i])) for i in range(gs.size) if w[i]>1e-12]
```
No lambdas. K maybe adaptive. This selects top K but if many equal, argpartition arbitrary; okay. For exact max, K=1 temp=0.

`def gradient_from_items(h, items):` loops as above.

`def fw_direction(grad, n):`

SLP direction implementation:
```
def slp_d(h, g, top_m=None):
   n=len(h)
   if top_m is None or top_m >= g.size: idxs=np.arange(g.size)
   else: idxs=np.argpartition(g, g.size-top_m)[-top_m:]
   A=np.empty((len(idxs), n+1), dtype=float)
   b=np.empty(len(idxs), dtype=float)
   for r, idx in enumerate(idxs):
       row = gradient_row_from_idx(h, idx)
       A[r,0] = -1.0
       A[r,1:] = row
       b[r] = -float(g[idx])
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[0]=1.0
   ub=np.zeros(n+1); ub[0]=np.inf; ub[1:]=1-h
   lb=np.zeros(n+1); lb[0]=0.0; lb[1:]=-h
   Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,1:]=1.0; beq=np.zeros(1)
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=(lb,ub), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
      if res.status == 0:
         d=res.x[1:]
         # reduce if tiny
         d -= d.mean()
         if np.max(np.abs(d)) > 1e-12: return d
   except: pass
   return None
```
But note `A_ub` rows with `-1` for z and row for d; objective min z. If g constraints for all lags, at h0 current z can be max g ~47. But b = -g_idx; constraint row·d - z ≤ -g_idx. For a low g_idx, e.g. g=10, at d=0: -z ≤ -10 => z≥10. So optimum z at least predicted active. Good. If direction lowers all active linearly, z can reduce. However the solutions can be extreme due multiple opt; line search handles. `def compute_g(h):`
```
n = h.size
return np.correlate(h.astype(float, copy=False), 1.0 - h, mode='full')
```
This allocates 1-h each time. Could compute with `h, 1-h` okay.

`def c5_from_g(g,n): return float(np.max(g) * (2.0 / n))`

`def evaluate(h):`
```
g=compute_g(h)
return float(g.max()*(2.0/h.size))
```
For time, compute `g` once and return both.
```
def c5_and_g(h):
   g=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); return float(np.max(g)*(2/h.size)), g
```

`def project_box_sum(v, target):`
```
v=np.asarray(v,float)
n=v.size
if target <=0: ...
if target >=n: ...
lo = float(v.min()) - 1.0
hi = float(v.max()) + 1.0
for _ in range(80):
   mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
   s=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
   if s > target: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
out=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0)
diff=target-out.sum()
if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
    if diff > 0:
        room=1.0-out
        idx=int(np.argmax(room))
        add=min(diff, room[idx])
        out[idx]+=add
        diff-=add
        if abs(diff)>1e-13:
           # distribute
           mask=room>0
           if mask.any(): out[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
    else:
        idx=int(np.argmax(out))
        sub=min(-diff, out[idx])
        out[idx]-=sub
        diff+=sub
        if abs(diff)>1e-13:
           mask=out>0
           if mask.any(): out[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
out=np.clip(out,0.0,1.0)
# final tiny adjust
diff=target-out.sum()
if abs(diff)>1e-14:
   if diff>0: idx=np.argmax(1-out); out[idx]=min(1, out[idx]+diff)
   else: idx=np.argmax(out); out[idx]=max(0, out[idx]+diff)
return out
```
If v has nan? not.

`def top_weight_items(g, K, temp):`
```
m=g.size
K=min(int(K), m)
maxg=float(np.max(g))
if K >= m:
   idx=np.arange(m)
else:
   idx=np.argpartition(g, m-K)
   idx=idx[m-K:]
gs=g[idx]
if temp <= 1e-300:
   w=np.full(gs.shape, 1.0/gs.size, dtype=float)
else:
   w=np.exp((gs-maxg)/float(temp))
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): w=np.full(...); else w/=s
items=[]
for ii in range(gs.size):
   ww=float(w[ii])
   if ww > 1e-12:
      items.append((int(idx[ii]), ww))
return items
```
If top_K includes all with temp small, okay.

`def gradient_from_items(h, items):`
```
n=h.size
grad=np.zeros(n,float)
h1=1.0-h
for idx,w in items:
   k = idx - (n-1)
   if k >= 0:
      L = n - k
      if L > 0:
         grad[k:] += w * h1[:L]
         grad[:L] -= w * h[k:]
   else:
      p = -k
      L = n - p
      if L > 0:
         grad[:L] += w * h1[p:]
         grad[p:] -= w * h[:L]
return grad
```
Check for k=0, L=n, grad all +=1-h, grad all -=h ->1-2h.
For k positive, first i=k..n-1: grad[k:] +=1-h[0..L-1]; second i=0..L-1: grad[:L]-=h[k..]. Good.
If weights sum not exactly 1 due cutoff, gradient of weighted sum with weights sum maybe <1; okay if small omitted. Could normalize? items weights normalized over selected top; if drop small, sum ~1. Fine.

`def gradient_row(h, idx):` similar single, no weight. Could use gradient_from_items with item? But allocation. Implement direct.
```
def gradient_row(h, idx):
   n=h.size; row=np.zeros(n); h1=1-h; k=idx-(n-1); ... return row
```

`def fw_direction(h, grad, target):`
```
n=h.size
order=np.argsort(grad)
v=np.zeros(n)
tf=float(target)
if tf <=0: return v-h? but sum 0.
full=int(math.floor(tf))
frac=tf-full
if full>=n: v.fill(1); return v-h
v[order[:full]]=1.0
if frac > 1e-15 and full < n:
   v[order[full]]=frac
d=v-h
d -= d.mean()
return d
```
If target fractional 125.5, first 125 ones, one 0.5; sum 125.5. Good. But in optimistic objective, linear minimization of grad·v with continuous v box/sum would set one fractional; yes.

`def projection_direction(h, grad, target, alpha):`
```
if alpha ==0: return None
htemp=project_box_sum(h - alpha*grad, target)
d=htemp-h
d -= d.mean()
return d
```

   cs = np.max(vals, axis=1)*dx

# refine around best_lam with one grid step

else:

... evaluate, update

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
   t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-5
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   # get initial
   try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
   except Exception: n0=250; h0=make_default(n0)
   if n0 < 20: n0=250; h0=make_default(n0)
   target=n0/2.0
   h0=project_box_sum(h0,target)
   c0,g0=eval_h(h0)
   best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c0
   # Phase 1: local on n0 until deadline1
   dl1 = t0 + 0.55 * (budget_s - 5)

   # maybe try n1 = max(n0*2, 400) capped 700

Detailed helper code:

```
import time
import math
import numpy as np
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def compute_g(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def eval_h(h):
    n = h.size
    g = compute_g(h)
    c = float(np.max(g) * (2.0 / n))
    return c, g

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    n = v.size
    if target <= 0.0:
        v = np.minimum(v, 0.0); return np.clip(v,0,1)
    if target >= n:
        v = np.maximum(v, 1.0); return np.clip(v,0,1)
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    out = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(out.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - out
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            add = min(diff, room[idx])
            out[idx] += add
            diff -= add
            if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
                mask = room > 1e-15
                if mask.any():
                    out[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
        else:
            mass = out.copy()
            idx = int(np.argmax(mass))
            sub = min(-diff, mass[idx])
            out[idx] -= sub
            diff += sub
            if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
                mask = mass > 1e-15
                if mask.any():
                    out[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final exact adjust
    diff = target - float(out.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - out))
            out[idx] = min(1.0, out[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(out))
            out[idx] = max(0.0, out[idx] + diff)
    return out
```

Check final exact diff: if diff positive and room idx has 0, min keeps within; if cannot adjust, sum still off tiny. Good.

`line_search` code with chunking:
```
def line_search(h, d, base_g=None, dx=None, grid=0):
    n = int(h.size)
    if dx is None: dx = 2.0 / n
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float).copy()
    d -= d.mean()
    if base_g is None:
        base_g = compute_g(h)
    base_c = float(np.max(base_g) * dx)
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if maxd < 1e-14:
        return h.copy(), base_c
    # feasible upper
    up = 1.0
    pos = d > 1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
        cand = ((1.0 - h)[pos] / d[pos]).min()
        if cand < up: up = float(cand)
    neg = d < -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
        cand = ((-h)[neg] / d[neg]).min()
        if cand < up: up = float(cand)
    if up <= 1e-12:
        return h.copy(), base_c
    if up > 1.0:
        up = 1.0
    if grid <= 0:
        if n <= 300: grid=768
        elif n <= 700: grid=448
        else: grid=288
    U = 1.0 - h
    g1 = np.correlate(d, U, mode='full') - np.correlate(h, d, mode='full')
    g2 = np.correlate(d, d, mode='full')
    best_c = base_c
    best_lam = 0.0
    # full grid
    lams = np.linspace(0.0, up, grid)
    m = base_g.size
    max_elems = 1000000
    chunk = max(1, min(grid, max_elems // max(1, m)))
    for start in range(0, grid, chunk):
        ls = lams[start:start+chunk]
        l2 = ls * ls
        vals = base_g[None, :] + g1[None, :] * ls[:, None] - g2[None, :] * l2[:, None]
        cs = np.max(vals, axis=1) * dx
        i = int(np.argmin(cs))
        if cs[i] < best_c:
            best_c = float(cs[i]); best_lam = float(ls[i])
    # refine
    step = up / max(1, grid - 1)
    if best_lam <= 1e-12:
        lo=0.0; hi=min(up, 3*step)
    elif best_lam >= up - 1e-12:
        lo=max(0.0, up-3*step); hi=up
    else:
        lo=max(0.0, best_lam-2*step); hi=min(up, best_lam+2*step)
    if hi > lo:
       lams2=np.linspace(lo,hi,256)
       ...
       update
       # second refine around new best with 128
       step2=hi-lo? maybe not needed. Could do one more:
       if 0<best_lam<up:
          lo2=max(0,best_lam-0.5*step); hi2=min(up,best_lam+0.5*step)
          lams3=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,128); update
    h_new = h + best_lam*d
    # ensure box lightly
    if np.any(h_new < -1e-12) or np.any(h_new > 1+1e-12):
       h_new = np.clip(h_new,0,1)
    return h_new, best_c
```
But if up<1 and best_lam=up, refine includes hi=up. If `up` tiny (5e-13) we return. Good.

`slp_direction` code. Need avoid top-level global `linprog` None.

```
def slp_direction(h, g, top_m=0):
    if linprog is None: return None
    n=h.size; m=g.size
    if top_m <= 0 or top_m >= m: idxs=np.arange(m)
    else: idxs=np.argpartition(g, m-top_m)[-top_m:]
    idxs = idxs[np.argsort(g[idxs])[::-1]]? Not necessary but deterministic.
    A = np.empty((idxs.size, n+1), dtype=float)
    b = np.empty(idxs.size, dtype=float)
    for r, idx in enumerate(idxs):
       row=gradient_row(h,int(idx))
       A[r,0] = -1.0
       A[r,1:] = row
       b[r] = -float(g[idx])
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[0]=1.0
    lb = np.empty(n+1); ub=np.empty(n+1)
    lb[0]=0.0; ub[0]=np.inf
    lb[1:] = -h
    ub[1:] = 1.0 - h
    Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,1:] = 1.0
    beq = np.zeros(1)
    try:
       res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=(lb,ub), method='highs')
       if res.status != 0: return None
       d = res.x[1:].copy()
       d -= d.mean()
       d[np.abs(d) < 1e-13] = 0.0
       if np.max(np.abs(d)) < 1e-12: return None
       return d
    except Exception:
       return None
```
Potential: `ub[0]=np.inf` with linprog okay. If `linprog` returns d with infeasible due numeric, line_search computes up; okay.

def local_block(h_start, best_c_start, t_end, rng, slp_every=7.0):
    h = np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size; target = 0.5*n
    # ensure feasible
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    c = compute maybe eval
    work_h = h.copy(); work_c = c; best_h=work_h.copy(); best_c=work_c
    stag=0; last_slp=time.time()-10
    scheme_sets = [ ... ] # top-level constants? Could define inside using no closure okay.
    while time.time() < t_end:
        if time.time() > t_end: break
        # if work_h infeasible? project
        if work_c > best_c + 0.002 and stag > 20:
            work_h=best_h.copy(); work_c=best_c
        g = compute_g(work_h)
        cur_c = float(np.max(g) * (2.0/n))
        if cur_c < work_c:
            work_c = cur_c
        if cur_c < best_c:
            best_c = cur_c; best_h = work_h.copy()
        # If close to zero? no
        improved = False
        set_idx = 0 if stag < 3 else (stag % 4)
        schemes = scheme_sets[set_idx]
        for K, temp in schemes:
            items = top_weight_items(g, K, temp)
            if not items: continue
            grad = gradient_from_items(work_h, items)
            nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if nrm < 1e-13: continue
            # direction 1 FW
            d = fw_direction(work_h, grad, target)
            htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=2.0/n)
            if ctry < work_c - 1e-13:
                htry = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                cex, _ = eval_h(htry)
                if cex < work_c - 1e-14:
                    work_h = htry; work_c = cex
                    if work_c < best_c: best_c=work_c; best_h=work_h.copy()
                    improved=True; break
            # direction projection
            base_alpha = 0.1 / max(nrm, 1e-12)
            for rel in (0.5, 2.0):
                if time.time() > t_end: break
                htemp = project_box_sum(work_h - base_alpha*rel*grad, target)
                d = htemp - work_h
                htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=2.0/n)
                if ctry < work_c - 1e-13:
                    htry = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                    cex, _ = eval_h(htry)
                    if cex < work_c - 1e-14:
                        work_h=htry; work_c=cex; if <best...; improved=True; break
            if improved: break
        if time.time() >= t_end: break
        if not improved:
            if time.time() - last_slp > slp_every and t_end - time.time() > 8:
                d = slp_direction(work_h, g, top_m=0 if n <= 350 else min(450, g.size))
                last_slp = time.time()
                if d is not None:
                    htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=2.0/n)
                    if ctry < work_c - 1e-13:
                        htry = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                        cex,_=eval_h(htry)
                        if cex < work_c - 1e-14:
                            work_h=htry; work_c=cex
                            if work_c < best_c: ...
                            stag=0
                            continue
            stag += 1
            if stag > 25:
                # Perturb work around best, but not if time low
                scale = 0.015 + 0.005 * min(10, stag // 10)
                hnew = project_box_sum(best_h + scale * rng.randn(n), target)
                cnew,_=eval_h(hnew)
                # Sometimes also try a huge default shape? maybe no
                work_h = hnew; work_c = cnew
                stag = 0
                # If perturbed c is very bad, maybe set work to best after immediate? We'll allow.
                if work_c > best_c + 0.01:
                    # to avoid wandering, maybe if cnew too bad set work=best_h and continue? But then no perturbation. Instead set work=best_h for now? hmm.
                    pass
    return best_h, best_c
```

Now, default h if no initial:
```
def make_default_h(n):
   x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
   # use a slightly trapezoid decreasing to reduce C; maybe a=0.25
   a = 0.2
   b = 2.0 - a
   h = np.zeros(n)
   h[x <= a] = 1.0
   mask = (x > a) & (x < b)
   h[mask] = 1.0 - (x[mask] - a) / (b - a)
   return h
```

`run` skeleton:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    B=max(1.0, float(budget_s)-5.0)
    deadline=t0+B
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if h0.size < 16:
          raise ValueError
       n0=h0.size
    except Exception:
       n0=250; h0=make_default_h(n0)
    h0=project_box_sum(h0, 0.5*n0)
    best_c,best_g=eval_h(h0)
    best_h=h0
    # quick initial: also evaluate a default/trapezoid at same n maybe? If parent bad, could compare.
    # But initial should be good. We can make alternative ramp/triangle and choose best before local.
    for alt in (make_default_h(n0), make_triangle(n0)):
       ha=project_box_sum(alt,0.5*n0)
       ca,_=eval_h(ha)
       if ca<best_c: best_h=ha; best_c=ca
    # Local phases
    target_bound=0.3808
    def? no closures. Use helper `maybe_finalize`? No closures allowed? We can just code inline.
    dl1 = t0 + 0.40*B
    if deadline - time.time() > 5:
       best_h,best_c = local_block(best_h, best_c, min(deadline-5, dl1), rng)
       if best_c <= target_bound: return project...
    # fine
    n_fine = int(round(min(512, max(320, 1.6*n0))))
    if n_fine != n0 and time.time() < deadline - 0.25*B:
       h_start = interpolate_to_n(best_h, n_fine)
       c_start,_=eval_h(h_start)
       # Also maybe start from alt ramp at n_fine if c_start much worse? Interp best likely.
       best_f,best_cf = local_block(h_start, c_start, min(deadline-20, t0+0.68*B), rng)
       if best_cf + 1e-12 < best_c:
          best_h=best_f; best_c=best_cf
       if best_c <= target_bound: return...
    # further fine 512
    if time.time() < deadline - 0.12*B and 512 not tried and 512 > n_fine:
       ... until t0+0.80*B
    # coarse
    n_coarse = int(round(max(96, 0.8*n0)))
    if n_coarse not in tried and time.time() < deadline - 0.06*B:
       ... until t0+0.88*B
    # another coarse 0.65
    ... until 0.93*B
    # final polish best
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       best_h,best_c=local_block(best_h,best_c, deadline-3, rng)
    h=project_box_sum(best_h,0.5*best_h.size)
    c,g=eval_h(h)
    if c < best_c: best_c=c
    # If due projection c > original? use c (exact). If c > best_c? best_c not updated; set best_c=c.
    return h, c, best_h.size
```

Pseudo refined local_block:
```
def local_block(h_start, best_c_start, t_end, rng):
    h = project...
    n=h.size; target=0.5*n; dx=2/n
    work_h=h; work_c=eval_h(h)[0]
    best_h=work_h.copy(); best_c=work_c
    if best_c_start < best_c:
        best_c=best_c_start? Wait if start work_h has c=best_c_start; so same. If best_c_start not equal due float, min.
    stag=0; last_slp=time.time()-10
    scheme_sets=...
    while time.time() < t_end:
        if work_c > best_c + 0.005 and stag > 15:
            work_h=best_h.copy(); work_c=best_c
        g=compute_g(work_h)
        cur_c=float(np.max(g)*dx)
        if cur_c < work_c: work_c=cur_c
        if cur_c < best_c: best_c=cur_c; best_h=work_h.copy()
        improved=False
        set_idx = 0 if stag < 3 else stag % len(scheme_sets)
        for K,temp in scheme_sets[set_idx]:
            if time.time() > t_end: break
            items = top_weight_items(g,K,temp)
            if not items: continue
            grad=gradient_from_items(work_h,items)
            nrm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if nrm < 1e-13: continue
            # FW
            d=fw_direction(work_h,grad,target)
            if np.max(np.abs(d)) > 1e-13:
               htry,ctry=line_search(work_h,d,base_g=g,dx=dx)
               if ctry < work_c - 1e-13:
                   htry=project_box_sum(htry,target)
                   cex,_=eval_h(htry)
                   if cex < work_c - 1e-14:
                      work_h=htry; work_c=cex; if work_c<best_c: best_c=cex; best_h=work_h.copy()
                      improved=True; break
            base_alpha=0.1/max(nrm,1e-12)
            for rel in (0.5, 2.0, 5.0):
               if time... break
               htemp=project_box_sum(work_h - base_alpha*rel*grad,target)
               d=htemp-work_h
               if max(abs(d))<1e-13: continue
               htry,ctry=line_search(work_h,d,base_g=g,dx=dx)
               if ctry < work_c - 1e-13:
                   htry=project_box_sum(htry,target)
                   cex,_=eval_h(htry)
                   if cex < work_c -1e-14:
                       work_h=htry; work_c=cex; update; improved=True; break
            if improved: break
        if time.time() >= t_end: break
        if not improved:
            if time.time() - last_slp > 7.0 and t_end - time.time() > 10:
                top_m = g.size if n <= 350 else min(450, g.size)
                d=slp_direction(work_h,g,top_m)
                last_slp=time.time()
                if d is not None:
                   htry,ctry=line_search(work_h,d,base_g=g,dx=dx)
                   if ctry < work_c -1e-13:
                      htry=project_box_sum(htry,target)
                      cex,_=eval_h(htry)
                      if cex < work_c -1e-14:
                         work_h=htry; work_c=cex; update; stag=0; continue
            stag += 1
            if stag > 30:
                scale=0.012+0.004*min(15, stag//10)
                hnew=project_box_sum(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), target)
                cnew,_=eval_h(hnew)
                work_h=hnew; work_c=cnew
                stag=0
        # loop
    return best_h,best_c
```

Now computing g1/g2 by finite differences:
```
rp = 1e-4
hp = h + rp*d
hm = h - rp*d
gp = compute_g(hp)
gm = compute_g(hm)
a1 = (gp - gm) / (2*rp)
a2 = (gp + gm - 2*base_g) / (rp*rp)
# g(λ)=base_g + a1*λ + a2*λ^2
```

def line_search(h, d, base_g=None, dx=None, grid=0):
   n=h.size; dx=...
   d=np.asarray(d,float).copy(); d -= d.mean()
   maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
   if maxd < 1e-14: ...
   d = d / maxd
   if base_g is None: base_g=compute_g(h)
   # feasible up
   up=1.0
   pos=d>1e-14; if any: up=min(up, ((1-h)[pos]/d[pos]).min())
   neg=d<-1e-14; if any: up=min(up, ((-h)[neg]/d[neg]).min())
   if up <= 1e-10: return h.copy(), max(base_c)
   if up > 1.0: up=1.0
   rho = 1e-4
   # Need if up < rho maybe smaller? Use smaller rho = max(1e-7, min(1e-4, up*0.1))? If up extremely small, h±rho*d may still slightly out; okay. But derivative computed at h independent of up. Keep 1e-4.
   hp = h + rho*d
   hm = h - rho*d
   gp = compute_g(hp); gm=compute_g(hm)
   a1 = (gp - gm)/(2*rho)
   a2 = (gp + gm - 2.0*base_g)/(rho*rho)
   # grid evaluate base + a1 λ + a2 λ²
```

def local_block(h_start, c_start, t_end, rng):
    h0 = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h_start,float).copy(), 0.5*n)
    n = h0.size; target=0.5*n; dx=2/n
    work_h = h0.copy()
    work_c = c_start
    best_h = work_h.copy(); best_c = min(work_c, c_start)
    # if c_start not eval, ensure
    wc , _ = eval_h(work_h)
    work_c = wc; best_c = wc
    no_improve=0; bad_count=0; last_slp=time.time()-100
    schemes = (...)
    while time.time() < t_end:
       if work_c > best_c + 1e-7:
           bad_count += 1? no maybe after no improv.
       g=compute_g(work_h); cur_c=...
       if cur_c < work_c: work_c=cur_c
       if cur_c < best_c: best_c=cur_c; best_h=work_h.copy()
       improved=False
       set_idx=... based on no_improve
       for ...:
          ...
          if exact cex < work_c - 1e-14:
              work_h=htry; work_c=cex
              if cex < best_c: best_c=cex; best_h=work_h.copy()
              improved=True
              if work_c > best_c + 1e-7: bad_count +=1
              else: bad_count=0
              break
       if time... break
       if improved:
          no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          bad_count +=1
          # SLP...
          if SLP improved: ... no_improve=0; continue
          if no_improve > 25 or bad_count>120:
              if no_improve > 25:
                  scale=...
                  hnew=project(box, best_h + scale*randn)
                  cnew=eval
                  work_h=hnew; work_c=cnew
                  no_improve=0
              if bad_count>120:
                  work_h=best_h.copy(); work_c=best_c; bad_count=0; no_improve=0
    return best_h,best_c
```

Let's code top items:
```
def top_weight_items(g,K,temp):
  m=g.size; K=min(K,m); maxg=float(g.max())
  if temp <= 0:
      close = np.where(g >= maxg - 1e-9)[0]
      if close.size > 0 and close.size <= max(K,1): idx=close
      else:

```
def interpolate_to_n(h, n_target):
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    if h.size == n_target: return h.copy()
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,h.size)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_target)
    hnew=np.interp(xn,xo,h)
    return project_box_sum(hnew,0.5*n_target)
```

Review `line_search` final code:
```
def line_search(h, d, base_g=None, dx=None, grid=0):
    n=h.size
    if dx is None: dx=2.0/n
    d=np.asarray(d,float).copy()
    d -= d.mean()
    if base_g is None: base_g=compute_g(h)
    maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if maxd < 1e-14:
        return h.copy(), float(np.max(base_g)*dx)
    d = d / maxd
    up=1.0
    pos=d>1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
       val=float(np.min((1.0-h)[pos]/d[pos]))
       if val < up: up=val
    neg=d< -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
       val=float(np.min((-h)[neg]/d[neg]))
       if val < up: up=val
    base_c = float(np.max(base_g)*dx)
    if up <= 1e-10:
       return h.copy(), base_c
    if up > 1.0: up=1.0
    rho=1e-4
    hp=h+rho*d; hm=h-rho*d
    gp=compute_g(hp); gm=compute_g(hm)
    a1=(gp-gm)/(2.0*rho)
    a2=(gp+gm-2.0*base_g)/(rho*rho)
    if grid <=0:
       if n <= 300: grid=768
       elif n <= 700: grid=448
       else: grid=320
    best_c=base_c; best_lam=0.0
    lams=np.linspace(0.0, up, grid)
    m=base_g.size
    max_elems=1200000
    chunk=max(1, min(grid, max_elems // max(1,m)))
    for start in range(0, grid, chunk):
       ls=lams[start:start+chunk]
       l2=ls*ls
       vals=base_g[None,:] + a1[None,:]*ls[:,None] + a2[None,:]*l2[:,None]
       cs=np.max(vals, axis=1)*dx
       i=int(np.argmin(cs))
       if cs[i] < best_c:
           best_c=float(cs[i]); best_lam=float(ls[i])
    step=up/max(1, grid-1)
    if best_lam <= 1e-12:
       lo=0.0; hi=min(up, 3.0*step)
    elif best_lam >= up - 1e-12:
       lo=max(0.0, up-3.0*step); hi=up
    else:
       lo=max(0.0, best_lam-2.0*step); hi=min(up, best_lam+2.0*step)
    if hi > lo:
       lams2=np.linspace(lo,hi,256)
       l2b=lams2*lams2
       vals=base_g[None,:] + a1[None,:]*lams2[:,None] + a2[None,:]*l2b[:,None]
       cs=np.max(vals,axis=1)*dx
       i=int(np.argmin(cs))
       if cs[i] < best_c:
           best_c=float(cs[i]); best_lam=float(lams2[i])
       # second refine around best if interior
       if 0.0 < best_lam < up:
          lo2=max(0.0, best_lam-0.5*step); hi2=min(up, best_lam+0.5*step)
          if hi2 > lo2:
             lams3=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,128)
             l2c=lams3*lams3
             vals=...
             cs=...
             i=...
             if cs[i] < best_c: best_c=...; best_lam=...
    h_new = h + best_lam*d
    # if tiny outside clip? For line formula, maybe don't clip. But for line_search return, we can clip tiny.
    if np.min(h_new) < -1e-12 or np.max(h_new) > 1.0+1e-12:
       h_new = np.clip(h_new,0.0,1.0)
    return h_new, best_c
```
If `up` very small, grid step small, refine hi/lo okay. If `up=0`, returned earlier.

Potential issue: `a2` may be inaccurate if `base_g` not exactly same as compute_g(h) due passed stale? We pass current. Good.

Now, `fw_direction`: If target fractional and full could exceed n? target 0.5n. For n=1 target 0.5; full=0, frac=0.5; order [0], v[0]=0.5. Good. `d = v - h`, subtract mean (0 since sums equal) but target fractional maybe sum exactly? v sum target. Good.

```

Now, `local_block` uses `best_h` for perturb. If `work_c` is already updated to `best_c`, `best_h` current. Good.

Let's write local_block exact code to avoid missing variables.

```
def local_block(h_start, t_end, rng):
    h_start = np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(h_start.size)
    target = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n
    work_h = project_box_sum(h_start, target)
    work_c, _ = eval_h(work_h)
    best_h = work_h.copy()
    best_c = work_c
    no_improve = 0
    bad_count = 0
    last_slp = time.time() - 100.0
    slp_interval = 12.0 if n <= 350 else 30.0
    temp_scale = max(1.0, min(3.0, float(n) / 250.0))
    scheme_sets = ...
    while time.time() < t_end:
        if work_h.size != n: ... no
        g = compute_g(work_h)
        cur_c = float(np.max(g) * dx)
        if cur_c < work_c:
            work_c = cur_c
        if cur_c < best_c:
            best_c = cur_c
            best_h = work_h.copy()
        if best_c == best_c and bad_count > 0 and work_c <= best_c + 1e-8:
            bad_count = 0
        improved = False
        set_idx = 0
        if no_improve >= 3:
            set_idx = no_improve % len(scheme_sets)
        for K_base, temp_base in scheme_sets[set_idx]:
            if time.time() >= t_end:
                break
            temp = temp_base * temp_scale
            items = top_weight_items(g, K_base, temp)
            if not items:
                continue
            grad = gradient_from_items(work_h, items)
            nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if nrm < 1e-13:
                continue
            d = fw_direction(work_h, grad, target)
            if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-13:
                htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=dx)
                if ctry < work_c + 1e-10:
                    hproj = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                    cex, _ = eval_h(hproj)
                    if cex < work_c - 1e-13:
                        work_h = hproj
                        work_c = cex
                        if cex < best_c:
                            best_c = cex
                            best_h = work_h.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
            base_alpha = 0.1 / max(nrm, 1e-12)
            for rel in (1.0, 4.0):
                if time.time() >= t_end:
                    break
                htemp = project_box_sum(work_h - base_alpha * rel * grad, target)
                d = htemp - work_h
                if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) < 1e-13:
                    continue
                htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=dx)
                if ctry < work_c + 1e-10:
                    hproj = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                    cex, _ = eval_h(hproj)
                    if cex < work_c - 1e-13:
                        work_h = hproj
                        work_c = cex
                        if cex < best_c:
                            best_c = cex
                            best_h = work_h.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
            if improved:
                break
        if time.time() >= t_end:
            break
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            if work_c > best_c + 1e-7:
                bad_count += 1
            else:
                bad_count = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            bad_count += 1
            if time.time() - last_slp > slp_interval and t_end - time.time() > 20.0:
                if n <= 350:
                    top_m = g.size
                else:
                    top_m = min(500, g.size)
                d = slp_direction(work_h, g, top_m)
                last_slp = time.time()
                if d is not None:
                    htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=dx)
                    if ctry < work_c + 1e-10:
                        hproj = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                        cex, _ = eval_h(hproj)
                        if cex < work_c - 1e-13:
                            work_h = hproj
                            work_c = cex
                            if cex < best_c:
                                best_c = cex
                                best_h = work_h.copy()
                            no_improve = 0
                            if work_c <= best_c + 1e-8:
                                bad_count = 0
                            else:
                                bad_count += 1
                            continue
            if bad_count > 160:
                work_h = best_h.copy()
                work_c = best_c
                no_improve = 0
                bad_count = 0
            elif no_improve > 25:
                scale = 0.012 + 0.003 * min(10.0, no_improve / 10.0)
                hnew = project_box_sum(best_h + scale * rng.randn(n), target)
                cnew, _ = eval_h(hnew)
                work_h = hnew
                work_c = cnew
                no_improve = 0
                bad_count = 0
    return best_h, best_c
```

def try_resolution(best_h, best_c, n_target, t_end, rng):
    if n_target < 16 or t_end - time.time() < 15.0:
        return best_h, best_c
    h_start = interpolate_to_n(best_h, n_target)
    h_best, c_best = local_block(h_start, t_end, rng)
    if c_best + 1e-12 < best_c:
        return h_best, c_best
    return best_h, best_c
```

def finalize_solution(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)

    c,_=eval_h(h)
    return h,c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    B=max(1.0, float(budget_s)-5.0)
    deadline=t0+B
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
       if h0.ndim != 1 or h0.size < 16:
           raise ValueError
       n0=int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
       n0=250
       h0=make_default_h(n0)
    h0=project_box_sum(h0,0.5*n0)
    best_c,_=eval_h(h0)
    best_h=h0
    # alternatives
    h_alt=project_box_sum(make_default_h(n0),0.5*n0); c_alt,_=eval_h(h_alt); if c_alt<best_c: ...
    h_alt=project_box_sum(make_triangle_h(n0),0.5*n0); ...
    done=[n0]
    # phase1
    ph1=t0+0.40*B
    if ph1 - time.time() > 10:
       best_h,best_c=local_block(best_h, min(deadline-5, ph1), rng)
    # ensure best_h size maybe changed? local_block same n.
    # fine1
    nf1=nearest_even(min(512, max(n0+24, 1.5*n0)))
    ph2=t0+0.66*B
    if nf1 not in done and ph2 - time.time() > 20:
       best_h,best_c=try_resolution(best_h,best_c,nf1,min(deadline-10,ph2),rng)
       done.append(nf1)
    nf2=nearest_even(min(512, max(nf1+24, 2*n0)))
    ph3=t0+0.80*B
    if nf2 not in done and ph3 - time.time() > 20:
       best_h,best_c=try_resolution(best_h,best_c,nf2,min(deadline-10,ph3),rng)
       done.append(nf2)
    nc1=nearest_even(max(96, 0.8*n0))
    ph4=t0+0.87*B
    if nc1 not in done and ph4 - time.time() > 15:
       best_h,best_c=try_resolution(best_h,best_c,nc1,min(deadline-8,ph4),rng)
       done.append(nc1)
    nc2=nearest_even(max(80, 0.65*n0))
    ph5=t0+0.93*B
    if nc2 not in done and ph5 - time.time() > 10:
       best_h,best_c=try_resolution(best_h,best_c,nc2,min(deadline-6,ph5),rng)
       done.append(nc2)
    # final polish
    if deadline - time.time() > 8:
       best_h,best_c=local_block(best_h, min(deadline-3, deadline), rng)
    h,c=finalize_solution(best_h)
    return h,c

```python
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def compute_g(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def eval_h(h):
    n = int(h.size)
    g = compute_g(h)
    c = float(np.max(g) * (2.0 / n))
    return c, g


def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.clip(np.minimum(v, 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    if target >= float(n):
        return np.clip(np.maximum(v, 0.0), 0.0, 1.0)

    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    out = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(out.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - out
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            add = min(diff, float(room[idx]))
            out[idx] += add
            diff -= add
            if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
                mask = room > 1e-15
                if mask.any():
                    out[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        else:
            mass = out.copy()
            idx = int(np.argmax(mass))
            sub = min(-diff, float(mass[idx]))
            out[idx] -= sub
            diff += sub
            if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
                mask = mass > 1e-15
                if mask.any():
                    out[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(out.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - out))
            out[idx] = min(1.0, float(out[idx]) + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(out))
            out[idx] = max(0.0, float(out[idx]) + diff)

    return out


def top_weight_items(g, K, temp):
    m = int(g.size)
    K = int(min(max(1, K), m))
    maxg = float(np.max(g))

    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(g, m - K)[-K:]

    if temp <= 0.0:
        close = np.where(g >= maxg - 1e-9)[0]
        if close.size > 0 and close.size <= max(1, K):
            idx = close
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(g, m - K)[-K:]
        w = np.full(idx.shape, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=float)
    else:
        gs = g[idx]
        w = np.exp((gs - maxg) / float(temp))
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.full(idx.shape, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=float)
        else:
            w = w / s

    out = []
    for i in range(idx.size):
        ww = float(w[i])
        if ww > 1e-12:
            out.append((int(idx[i]), ww))
    return out


def gradient_from_items(h, items):
    n = int(h.size)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h1 = 1.0 - h

    for idx, w in items:
        k = idx - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            L = n - k
            if L > 0:
                grad[k:] += w * h1[:L]
                grad[:L] -= w * h[k:]
        else:
            p = -k
            L = n - p
            if L > 0:
                grad[:L] += w * h1[p:]
                grad[p:] -= w * h[:L]
    return grad


def gradient_row(h, idx):
    n = int(h.size)
    row = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h1 = 1.0 - h
    k = idx - (n - 1)
    if k >= 0:
        L = n - k
        if L > 0:
            row[k:] += h1[:L]
            row[:L] -= h[k:]
    else:
        p = -k
        L = n - p
        if L > 0:
            row[:L] += h1[p:]
            row[p:] -= h[:L]
    return row


def fw_direction(h, grad, target):
    n = int(h.size)
    order = np.argsort(grad)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    tf = float(target)

    full = int(min(n, max(0, int(tf))))
    frac = tf - float(full)

    if full > 0:
        v[order[:full]] = 1.0
    if frac > 1e-15 and full < n:
        v[order[full]] = frac

    d = v - h
    d -= d.mean()
    return d


def line_search(h, d, base_g=None, dx=None, grid=0):
    n = int(h.size)
    if dx is None:
        dx = 2.0 / n

    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float).copy()
    d -= d.mean()
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if maxd < 1e-14:
        if base_g is None:
            base_g = compute_g(h)
        return h.copy(), float(np.max(base_g) * dx)

    d = d / maxd

    if base_g is None:
        base_g = compute_g(h)

    base_c = float(np.max(base_g) * dx)

    up = 1.0
    pos = d > 1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
        val = float(np.min((1.0 - h)[pos] / d[pos]))
        if val < up:
            up = val
    neg = d < -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
        val = float(np.min((-h)[neg] / d[neg]))
        if val < up:
            up = val

    if up <= 1e-10:
        return h.copy(), base_c
    if up > 1.0:
        up = 1.0

    rho = 1e-4
    hp = h + rho * d
    hm = h - rho * d

    gp = compute_g(hp)
    gm = compute_g(hm)

    a1 = (gp - gm) / (2.0 * rho)
    a2 = (gp + gm - 2.0 * base_g) / (rho * rho)

    if grid <= 0:
        if n <= 300:
            grid = 768
        elif n <= 700:
            grid = 448
        else:
            grid = 320

    best_c = base_c
    best_lam = 0.0

    lams = np.linspace(0.0, up, grid)
    m = base_g.size
    max_elems = 1200000
    chunk = max(1, min(grid, max_elems // max(1, m)))

    for start in range(0, grid, chunk):
        ls = lams[start:start + chunk]
        l2 = ls * ls
        vals = base_g[None, :] + a1[None, :] * ls[:, None] + a2[None, :] * l2[:, None]
        cs = np.max(vals, axis=1) * dx
        i = int(np.argmin(cs))
        if cs[i] < best_c:
            best_c = float(cs[i])
            best_lam = float(ls[i])

    step = up / max(1, grid - 1)
    if best_lam <= 1e-12:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = min(up, 3.0 * step)
    elif best_lam >= up - 1e-12:
        lo = max(0.0, up - 3.0 * step)
        hi = up
    else:
        lo = max(0.0, best_lam - 2.0 * step)
        hi = min(up, best_lam + 2.0 * step)

    if hi > lo:
        lams2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 256)
        l2b = lams2 * lams2
        vals = base_g[None, :] + a1[None, :] * lams2[:, None] + a2[None, :] * l2b[:, None]
        cs = np.max(vals, axis=1) * dx
        i = int(np.argmin(cs))
        if cs[i] < best_c:
            best_c = float(cs[i])
            best_lam = float(lams2[i])

        if 0.0 < best_lam < up:
            lo2 = max(0.0, best_lam - 0.5 * step)
            hi2 = min(up, best_lam + 0.5 * step)
            if hi2 > lo2:
                lams3 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 128)
                l2c = lams3 * lams3
                vals = base_g[None, :] + a1[None, :] * lams3[:, None] + a2[None, :] * l2c[:, None]
                cs = np.max(vals, axis=1) * dx
                i = int(np.argmin(cs))
                if cs[i] < best_c:
                    best_c = float(cs[i])
                    best_lam = float(lams3[i])

    h_new = h + best_lam * d
    if np.min(h_new) < -1e-12 or np.max(h_new) > 1.0 + 1e-12:
        h_new = np.clip(h_new, 0.0, 1.0)

    return h_new, best_c


def slp_direction(h, g, top_m=0):
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    n = int(h.size)
    m = int(g.size)

    if top_m <= 0 or top_m >= m:
        idxs = np.arange(m)
    else:
        idxs = np.argpartition(g, m - top_m)[-top_m:]

    A = np.empty((idxs.size, n + 1), dtype=float)
    b = np.empty(idxs.size, dtype=float)

    for r, idx in enumerate(idxs):
        row = gradient_row(h, int(idx))
        A[r, 0] = -1.0
        A[r, 1:] = row
        b[r] = -float(g[idx])

    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c[0] = 1.0

    lb = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    ub = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    lb[0] = 0.0
    ub[0] = 1.0e6
    lb[1:] = -h
    ub[1:] = 1.0 - h

    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
    Aeq[0, 1:] = 1.0
    beq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            A_eq=Aeq,
            b_eq=beq,
            bounds=(lb, ub),
            method="highs",
        )
        if res.status != 0:
            return None
        d = np.asarray(res.x[1:], dtype=float).copy()
        d -= d.mean()
        d[np.abs(d) < 1e-13] = 0.0
        if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) < 1e-12:
            return None
        return d
    except Exception:
        return None


def make_default_h(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n))
    return np.clip(1.0 - 0.5 * x, 0.0, 1.0)


def make_triangle_h(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n))
    return np.clip(1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)


def nearest_even(v):
    iv = int(round(float(v) / 2.0) * 2)
    if iv < 16:
        iv = 16
    return iv


def interpolate_to_n(h, n_target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_target = int(n_target)
    if h.size == n_target:
        return h.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_target)
    hnew = np.interp(xn, xo, h)
    return project_box_sum(hnew, 0.5 * n_target)


def local_block(h_start, t_end, rng):
    h_start = np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(h_start.size)
    target = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n

    work_h = project_box_sum(h_start, target)
    work_c, _ = eval_h(work_h)

    best_h = work_h.copy()
    best_c = work_c

    no_improve = 0
    bad_count = 0
    last_slp = time.time() - 100.0
    slp_interval = 12.0 if n <= 350 else 30.0
    temp_scale = max(1.0, min(3.0, float(n) / 250.0))

    scheme_sets = (
        ((1, 0.0), (2, 0.0), (3, 0.0), (5, 0.1)),
        ((3, 0.0), (5, 0.05), (10, 0.2), (20, 0.5)),
        ((5, 0.05), (10, 0.1), (20, 0.3), (40, 0.8)),
        ((10, 0.1), (20, 0.2), (40, 0.5), (80, 1.2)),
    )

    while time.time() < t_end:
        g = compute_g(work_h)
        cur_c = float(np.max(g) * dx)

        if cur_c < work_c:
            work_c = cur_c
        if cur_c < best_c:
            best_c = cur_c
            best_h = work_h.copy()

        improved = False
        set_idx = 0
        if no_improve >= 3:
            set_idx = no_improve % len(scheme_sets)

        for K_base, temp_base in scheme_sets[set_idx]:
            if time.time() >= t_end:
                break

            temp = temp_base * temp_scale
            items = top_weight_items(g, K_base, temp)
            if not items:
                continue

            grad = gradient_from_items(work_h, items)
            nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if nrm < 1e-13:
                continue

            d = fw_direction(work_h, grad, target)
            if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-13:
                htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=dx)
                if ctry < work_c + 1e-10:
                    hproj = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                    cex, _ = eval_h(hproj)
                    if cex < work_c - 1e-13:
                        work_h = hproj
                        work_c = cex
                        if cex < best_c:
                            best_c = cex
                            best_h = work_h.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

            base_alpha = 0.1 / max(nrm, 1e-12)
            for rel in (1.0, 4.0):
                if time.time() >= t_end:
                    break

                htemp = project_box_sum(work_h - base_alpha * rel * grad, target)
                d = htemp - work_h
                if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) < 1e-13:
                    continue

                htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=dx)
                if ctry < work_c + 1e-10:
                    hproj = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                    cex, _ = eval_h(hproj)
                    if cex < work_c - 1e-13:
                        work_h = hproj
                        work_c = cex
                        if cex < best_c:
                            best_c = cex
                            best_h = work_h.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

            if improved:
                break

        if time.time() >= t_end:
            break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            if work_c > best_c + 1e-7:
                bad_count += 1
            else:
                bad_count = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            bad_count += 1

            if time.time() - last_slp > slp_interval and t_end - time.time() > 20.0:
                if n <= 350:
                    top_m = g.size
                else:
                    top_m = min(500, g.size)

                d = slp_direction(work_h, g, top_m)
                last_slp = time.time()

                if d is not None:
                    htry, ctry = line_search(work_h, d, base_g=g, dx=dx)
                    if ctry < work_c + 1e-10:
                        hproj = project_box_sum(htry, target)
                        cex, _ = eval_h(hproj)
                        if cex < work_c - 1e-13:
                            work_h = hproj
                            work_c = cex
                            if cex < best_c:
                                best_c = cex
                                best_h = work_h.copy()
                            no_improve = 0
                            if work_c <= best_c + 1e-8:
                                bad_count = 0
                            else:
                                bad_count += 1
                            continue

            if bad_count > 160:
                work_h = best_h.copy()
                work_c = best_c
                no_improve = 0
                bad_count = 0
            elif no_improve > 25:
                scale = 0.012 + 0.003 * min(10.0, float(no_improve) / 10.0)
                hnew = project_box_sum(best_h + scale * rng.randn(n), target)
                cnew, _ = eval_h(hnew)
                work_h = hnew
                work_c = cnew
                no_improve = 0
                bad_count = 0

    return best_h, best_c


def try_resolution(best_h, best_c, n_target, t_end, rng):
    n_target = int(n_target)
    if n_target < 16 or t_end - time.time() < 15.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    h_start = interpolate_to_n(best_h, n_target)
    h_best, c_best = local_block(h_start, t_end, rng)

    if c_best + 1e-12 < best_c:
        return h_best, c_best
    return best_h, best_c


def finalize_solution(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * h.size)
    c, _ = eval_h(h)
    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    deadline = t0 + B
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h0.ndim != 1 or h0.size < 16:
            raise ValueError
        n0 = int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 250
        h0 = make_default_h(n0)

    h0 = project_box_sum(h0, 0.5 * n0)
    best_c, _ = eval_h(h0)
    best_h = h0.copy()

    alt = project_box_sum(make_default_h(n0), 0.5 * n0)
    c_alt, _ = eval_h(alt)
    if c_alt < best_c:
        best_c = c_alt
        best_h = alt.copy()

    alt = project_box_sum(make_triangle_h(n0), 0.5 * n0)
    c_alt, _ = eval_h(alt)
    if c_alt < best_c:
        best_c = c_alt
        best_h = alt.copy()

    done = [int(best_h.size)]

    ph1 = t0 + 0.40 * B
    if ph1 - time.time() > 10.0:
        best_h, best_c = local_block(best_h, min(deadline - 5.0, ph1), rng)

    nf1 = nearest_even(min(512, max(int(best_h.size) + 24, int(1.5 * best_h.size))))
    ph2 = t0 + 0.66 * B
    if nf1 not in done and ph2 - time.time() > 20.0:
        best_h, best_c = try_resolution(
            best_h, best_c, nf1, min(deadline - 10.0, ph2), rng
        )
        done.append(int(best_h.size) if False else nf1)

    nf2 = nearest_even(min(512, max(nf1 + 24, int(2.0 * best_h.size))))
    ph3 = t0 + 0.80 * B
    if nf2 not in done and ph3 - time.time() > 20.0:
        best_h, best_c = try_resolution(
            best_h, best_c, nf2, min(deadline - 10.0, ph3), rng
        )
        done.append(int(best_h.size) if False else nf2)

    nc1 = nearest_even(max(96, int(0.8 * best_h.size)))
    ph4 = t0 + 0.87 * B
    if nc1 not in done and ph4 - time.time() > 15.0:
        best_h, best_c = try_resolution(
            best_h, best_c, nc1, min(deadline - 8.0, ph4), rng
        )
        done.append(int(best_h.size) if False else nc1)

    nc2 = nearest_even(max(80, int(0.65 * best_h.size)))
    ph5 = t0 + 0.93 * B
    if nc2 not in done and ph5 - time.time() > 10.0:
        best_h, best_c = try_resolution(
            best_h, best_c, nc2, min(deadline - 6.0, ph5), rng
        )
        done.append(int(best_h.size) if False else nc2)

    if deadline - time.time() > 8.0:
        best_h, best_c = local_block(best_h, max(deadline - 3.0, t0), rng)

    h_final, c_final = finalize_solution(best_h)
    return h_final, c_final, int(h_final.size)
```
